# Open bugs

Known behaviours that are kept on purpose for now. Each one has a name; reports refer to it by that name.
Fixing one is its own piece of work: write the failing test first, then change the code.

## Preserved (pinned by tests, not fixed)

Logged in extract session 1, batch V1, when `cloud/more-jini-1` was merged. The revert code now lives in
Jini `packages/cms/src/core/commands/revert.ts` (public entry `@jini-ai/cms/core/commands`); Tovu
imports it directly.

### `L079-force-without-principal-kind`

`revertChangeSet` called with `force: true` and no `principalKind` skips the stale version check, so a
revert can be applied over a row that changed after the change set was recorded. Only
`principalKind: "agent"` is refused under `force`.
Pinned by `apps/website/src/contracts/core/commands/__tests__/revert.unit.test.ts`,
"L079: force without principal kind bypasses stale versions (reported, not fixed)".

### `L079-partial-inverse-failure`

When a change set's inverses are applied in reverse order and a later inverse fails, the inverses
already applied stay applied. The revert is not all-or-nothing.
Pinned by the same file, "L079: a later inverse failure leaves earlier writes applied (reported, not fixed)".

## Stale documentation (text kept byte-exact)

### `snapshot-stale-header`

Jini `packages/db/src/data-modules/snapshot.ts`, the comment above `discardCommittedSnapshot`, says a
failed migration "leaves the entry non-terminal". The code marks the journal entry `ROLLED_BACK`, which
is terminal; the snapshot file itself is kept. The comment moved byte-exact and was not edited.

## Pre-existing test failures seen while checking other work

### `plugin-host-bundled-identity`

`apps/website/src/server/runtime/composition/__tests__/plugin-host.integration.test.ts`,
"every bundled agent and site package declares a distinct publisher identity and discovery terms",
fails with `unknown/charts-recharts` where `jini/charts-recharts-agent` is expected. The bundled Jini
manifests now declare their id under `extensions.jini`; the test reads identities with namespace `tovu`.
Seen in V1; it fails the same way on the V0 tip (before the merge), so the merge did not cause it.

## Resolved during extract session 2 continuation

### `A1-explicit-undefined-credential-metadata`

Moving the existing credential card builders and router into Jini exposed explicit `undefined`
properties accepted by Tovu's compiler but rejected by Jini's exact optional-property settings.
The development UI/agent-plugins builds demonstrated the type errors. The new UI test
`uses the host app identity and complete plugin metadata without introducing absent hints`
fails when the original hint mapping is restored (`hint: undefined`); the mutation was red.
The fix keeps absent hints absent, narrows always-present detail/description return types, and
explicitly permits already-supported undefined inputs in consumer-local loader/router types.
Card copy and submitted/saved data are unchanged. The corrected G-card fixture pins rendered parity.

## Incomplete at extract session 2 Continuation 2 stop

### `A2-resend-positional-http-abi`

The real bundled plugin calls positional `httpClient.send(request)` while the core port expects
`send({ request })`. New neutral-host `mail-port-regression.test.ts` demonstrated red with the
object-request fake (result.ok false). A draft `toPluginHttpClient` adapter makes that regression
green without editing the plugin. Mutation confirmation and the complete A2 gate were not run:
the required `parseSetup` change exposed a separate existing neutral-host test that needs wiring,
which triggers the brief's explicit stop rule. This entry is not a completed fix claim.

### `A2-resend-positional-http-abi` — Continuation 3 verification

The real plugin regression now passes through the public `toPluginHttpClient` adapter. Removing
the object-request wrapping from the adapter makes that regression red again; source bytes were
restored and platform rebuilt. The refusal and HTTP-header mutations each make the frozen
pre-move mail golden red. No plugin edits; no fixture regeneration. Full A2 results are recorded
in `cloud/reports/extract-s2.md`; this note supersedes the unfinished mutation status above.

## Found at combine-7 (Tovu `integ/merge-7-2026-11-20`, 2026-11-21) — listed, not fixed

### `C7-plugin-contribution-filenames`

Jini merge-7 (jy-pay K9) renamed every bundled agent-plugin contribution file from `tovu-*.json` to
`jini-*.json` (`cloudflare/jini-deploy-targets.json`, `github/jini-source-control.json`, …), but the
Tovu host still loads the old names through 14 filename constants (`DEPLOY_TARGETS_FILENAME`,
`DEPLOY_OPS_FILENAME`, `DEPLOY_CONFIGS_FILENAME`, `SOURCE_CONTROL_PROVIDERS_FILENAME`,
`MAIL_ADAPTERS_FILENAME`, `LOGIN_PROVIDERS_FILENAME`, `SEARCH_PROVIDERS_FILENAME`,
`OBJECT_STORES_FILENAME`, `CONSENT_CONTRIBUTIONS_FILENAME`, `API_CONNECTIONS_FILENAME`,
`AGENT_PLUGIN_TOOLS_FILENAME`, `ADMIN_SURFACES_FILENAME`, `CREDENTIAL_SCHEMES_FILENAME`,
`FORM_DESTINATIONS_FILENAME`), the three literals in `composition/plugin-credential-cards.ts:71-73`
and `site-backup/share-file-policy.ts:9`. Against Jini merge-7 the bundled plugins' contributions
do not load (e.g. `site-backup/__tests__/tool-registrations.unit.test.ts` 62/84 fail with ENOENT
`…/agent-plugins/github/tovu-source-control.json`). Red since the relink. Full list:
`cloud-results/combine-7-tovu-renamed-plugin-file-refs.txt`. Owner 11-20 accepted the rename; the
Tovu repoint (and whether third-party `tovu-*.json` keeps loading) is a decision.

### `C7-jini-directory-trash-exdev-cause`

tv-xd5-publish deleted Tovu's `features/trash/adapters/directory.ts` in favour of Jini's
`@jini-ai/cms/trash/adapters/directory` (`directory.node.ts:82`). Merge-6 had added
`{ cause: error }` to the cross-filesystem (`EXDEV`) error in the Tovu copy (04d030b5a); Jini's copy
throws without the cause, so the original errno is lost. Needs the fix in Jini.

### `C7-theme-preview-refresh-public-barrel`

`server/inbound/public-http/middleware/theme-preview-refresh.ts` (tv-xd5-publish) imports
`themeRosterPorts` from `#src/features/theme/index`, so the public request path loads feature-wide
barrels (`worker-import-graph.test.ts` new failing case "…theme-preview-refresh.ts keeps public reads
separate from feature-wide write/tool barrels").

### `C7-y1-credential-label-prefix`

tv-y1 T5 changed `server/runtime/composition/modules/site-search.ts` `SEARCH_CREDENTIAL_LABEL_PREFIX`
to `"Jini Search — "`; the prefix builds the credential label the search vendor lookup uses, so a site
that saved `Tovu Search — <vendor>` before may no longer resolve it (not verified end to end).
`site-search.unit.test.ts` ×2 still expect `Tovu Search — …`. Opposite drift in the same sheet:
`mail-provider-contribution.unit.test.ts` expects `Jini Mail — Resend API` while
`agent-plugins/mail-adapter-registry.ts:10 defaultCredentialLabel` still returns `Tovu Mail — …`.

  7b4fea84a) — the desktop renderer did not compile; fixed `8ec117a4a` together with the desktop's

- `@tovu/contracts` imported `@jini-ai/agent-runtime` undeclared (tv-ext-rs-c a52) — the packaged

## combine-8 (Tovu `integ/merge-8-2026-11-21`, 2026-11-21)

Found while merging combine-8 against Jini `integ/merge-8-2026-11-21` `3ad0c5671`. Listed, not fixed
(none blocks build or typecheck). Details: `cloud-results/combine-8-tovu.md`.

### `C8-github-managed-manifest-path` — confirmed (Jini rename, no migration)
- Jini `d9c625e6` changed the GitHub commit-site adapter's ownership manifest from
  `<folder>/.tovu/managed-files.json` to `<folder>/.jini/managed-files.json`
  (`packages/agent-plugins/github/source-control/commit-site.mjs:35`), with no read of the old path.
  A repository that Tovu already published to keeps its record under `.tovu/`; after the upgrade the
  adapter cannot see which files it owns (orphaned/unowned files on the next commit).
- Tovu `content/agent-plugin-overlays/github/GUIDANCE.md:78` still tells the agent `.tovu/managed-files.json`.
- Witness: `apps/website/src/features/agent-plugins/__tests__/unit/bundled-github-commit-site.unit.test.ts`
  goes 1 → 37 red now that tv-c7a makes the bundled plugin load (fetch `…/static_sites/.jini/managed-files.json`
  where the test expects `.tovu/`). Decision needed: dual-read/migrate in Jini, or accept and repoint the tests.

### `C8-dev-select-node-runtime-env` — confirmed (dev tooling)
- Jini merge-8 made `@jini-ai/devops/local-dev#selectNodeRuntime({ searchFrom, nodePath, env })` require
  `env` (the option default was removed). `development/scripts/dev.mjs:861` still passes only
  `{ searchFrom, nodePath }`, so the desktop-Electron probe spawns with `{ ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE: '1' }` and
  no PATH/HOME; a probe that needs them fails and `npm run dev` silently falls back to stock Node.
  `.mjs`, so no typecheck catches it. Fix: pass `env: process.env` (owed by the Jini combine-8 notes).

### `C8-css-unrenamed-jini-classes` — confirmed (visual, admin)
- Tovu styles Jini components through `.jini-*` selectors that Jini never emits (jy-cls skipped them):
  `apps/admin/src/integrations/jini-admin/jini-media.css:601` `.jini-tab-bar-tag` and `:613` `.jini-tab-bar-dot`
  (Jini `ui/…/tab-strip/react/components/TabBar.tsx:108,116` emits `tab-bar-dot` / `tab-bar-tag`), and `:781`
  `.jini-row-menu-popup-above` (Jini `admin/…/RowMenu/RowMenu.tsx:106` template literal ` row-menu-popup-above`).
  Those rules never match. Full reverse-check list (26 selectors): `cloud-results/combine-8-tovu-class-audit.txt`.

## combine-9 (Tovu `integ/merge-9-2026-11-21`, 2026-11-21)

Found while merging combine-9 against Jini `integ/merge-10-2026-11-21` `de4e555b9`. Listed, not fixed
(none blocks build or typecheck). Details: `cloud-results/combine-9-tovu.md`.

### `C9-share-zip-refusal-vs-acknowledgement` — confirmed (tv-sharezip T5 vs the existing acknowledgement flow)
- tv-sharezip T5 (`apps/website/src/features/site-backup/share-backend.ts`, `requireNoShareCredentialWarnings`,
  called right after both ZIP builds) now throws `SiteShareRefusal` `UNSAFE_FILE` whenever the ZIP collector
  records any credential warning. Before it, the same warnings produced a share with
  `needsAcknowledgement: true`, a 403 `ACKNOWLEDGE_CREDENTIALS` download block and a
  `POST …/site-shares/<id>/acknowledge` route. That whole acknowledgement path is now unreachable.
- Red on the tip, green on the base (merge-8 + lockfile pair): `features/site-backup/__tests__/credential-warnings.integration.test.ts`
  "export keeps database values and complete plugin packages; download requires a durable acknowledgement"
  (`SiteShareRefusal: The export contains files with possible credentials…`), and
  `features/site-backup/__tests__/integration/saved-keys-round-trip.integration.test.ts` "exports and imports contain
  no saved keys or inherited grants" (inSite) and (appData): the admin export route returns 400
  `{"code":"UNSAFE_FILE","message":"Export creation failed: The export contains files with possible credentials…"}`
  where the test expects 200.
- Effect: a site whose files carry anything credential-shaped can no longer be exported or shared at all;
  previously the owner could review and acknowledge. Decision: keep the hard refusal (then delete the
  acknowledgement route/state and repoint the 3 tests), or scope T5 to the public-token share and keep the
  acknowledged download for owner exports.

### `C9-site-chat-lockfile-stale` — confirmed (release/lockfile-1120 left one lockfile behind)
- `release/lockfile-1120` bumped `apps/site-chat/package.json` (`@jini-ai/agentic`, `chat`, `core`, `protocol`
  → `^0.7.3-next.1`) but did not regenerate `apps/site-chat/package-lock.json`, which still pins 0.7.0.
  `npm ci --ignore-scripts` in `apps/site-chat` fails: `EUSAGE … lock file's @jini-ai/agentic@0.7.0 does not
  satisfy @jini-ai/agentic@0.7.3-next.1` (also chat, core, protocol). Root/admin/desktop `npm ci`: 0.
  Fix: `npm install` in `apps/site-chat` against the published 0.7.3-next.1 and commit the lockfile.

## combine-10 (Tovu `integ/merge-10-2026-11-21`, 2026-11-21)

Follow-ups and cloud items on top of combine-9, against Jini `integ/merge-10-2026-11-21` `de4e555b9`.
Details: `cloud-results/combine-10-tovu.md`. **No new production bug found.**

- `C9-site-chat-lockfile-stale`: **FIXED** in `47dd8b157`. Only the `@jini-ai/*` entries were re-resolved
  to 0.7.3-next.1; every other locked version is unchanged. `npm ci --ignore-scripts` in `apps/site-chat`: 0.
- `C9-share-zip-refusal-vs-acknowledgement`: **still open**. Owner decision pending; tv-sharezip left as merged.
- Not production: the 3 tv-w2 I2-T media reds and the 3 lockfile-1120 pin reds were stale tests (repointed in
  `ab038aae8` and `75900acdf`). `byte-size.ts` still reports `null` for an unknown size.
- Test infrastructure (not production): with `TOVU_CHAT_TEST_POSTGRES_URL` set, every `*.dialects.test.ts`
  file shares one real Postgres database. `chat-dialect-matrix.ts` `emptiedPostgresChatKernel()` truncates the
  four `ai_chat.*` tables per make, so files running at the same time wipe each other's rows. Each file is green
  alone. Needs a per-process database/schema, or serial dialect files.

## combine-11 (Tovu `integ/merge-11-2026-11-21`, 2026-11-21)

Eleven merges on top of combine-10, against Jini `integ/merge-11-2026-11-21` `1ac1cbbd1` (published 0.7.3-next.2).
Details: `cloud-results/combine-11-tovu.md`.

### `C11-agui-run-error-raw-message` — OPEN (tv-ag1 × bugs1 T4)
- bugs1 T4 changed the catch in Tovu's own `drainAgUiStream`. On a dropped daemon stream the AG-UI `RUN_ERROR`
  carried the approved sentence "The assistant’s response was interrupted. Send your request again." in place
  of the transport text.
- tv-ag1 deleted that function, and Tovu now calls Jini `drainRunFramesToAgUi`. Its catch
  (`packages/chat/src/core/ag-ui/event-projection.ts:280` at `1ac1cbbd1`) writes `error.message` again.
- Witness: `assistant-ag-ui-wire.golden.test.ts` "a dropped daemon stream closes partial text and reports a
  private interruption message" fails with actual `'aborted'`.
- Fix options: a Jini change (a fixed message, or a host-supplied `interruptedMessage`), or a Tovu `write`
  wrapper that rewrites the catch-path `RUN_ERROR`. Not one line, so not fixed in combine-11.

### `C11-share-ack-403-admin` — FIXED (`c3ac7bd03`)
- bugs1 SHARE-ZIP moved `site-shares.ts` acknowledgement-required downloads from 409 to 403.
- The admin `use-share-credential-gate.hooks.ts:69` still opened the acknowledgement checkbox only for 409.
  The owner saw "Download failed." for a warned export.
- Fixed in one line (`!== 403`). The sheet's admin test is now 16/16.

### `C11-stage-payload-empty-refusal` — FIXED (`623bbbab7`), only on the merge
- main `d2edbcb43` added `failIfShellIsIncomplete` with `fail(string)`. The integ object-arg refactor's `fail`
  takes `{ message }`, so the refusal printed nothing (desktop tsc TS2345).
- Fixed in one line.

### `C11-installed-plugin-id-upgrade` — OPEN (known PARTIAL, now with a failing proof)
- Installed `tovu/<x>` bundled plugins from 0.7.2 (25 IDs) never reach `jini/<x>`. Canonical seeding keeps the
  old revision silently.
- Witness: `installed-plugin-id-upgrade.unit.test.ts` (red).

### Not production / listed
- `C10-A7` real-Postgres dialect contention: **FIXED** in test support (`1baf41c6f`).
- `vendor-neutrality` "no generic seam file names the first provider": 4 hits outside T-VENDOR's scope
  (`bundled-catalog.ts:47,48,57`, `shared-owner.ts:81`). This is BASE-RED and meets the T-VENDOR STOP.

- why-comments: 77 → 79 (`b3ec582b7325`, `096121b6167d`).

## Live-tree log (carried over from localhost-1103, 2026-11-10)

# Open bugs
Every bug any agent finds goes here the moment it's found, fixed or not, and the owner hears about it in the same reply.

**Rules (owner 10-31):**
1. Every fix starts with a FAILING test that reproduces the bug the way a user hits it: packaged app, real UI path, or a real file from another version. Show it red, fix, show it green.
2. Each bug names its CLASS, and the fix adds one guard for the whole class, not just this instance.
3. A line is removed only when the fix is merged AND verified where users hit it (packaged app / live site). The commit + test name go in the "Fixed" log at the bottom.

Format: found · who sees it · what breaks · class · status

## Release check: real client site (owner 10-31)
Before 0.1.26 is called working, entirely through the assistant chat:
1. In the mingjie admin chat, ask the assistant to export the site → save the zip to a temporary folder.
2. Create a new empty site → in its assistant chat, attach that zip and ask it to import the site.
3. Check that the new site matches: pages, posts, media, theme, the hero YouTube video, forms, plugin on/off; no refusals.
4. Repeat with "Include saved keys" on + the password → keys work in the new site.
5. Afterwards delete the temp zip (client data) and keep the new site until the owner has looked, then Trash it.
Run it on the fixed code: localhost on main, then the packaged 0.1.26.

## Bug classes (what these are teaching us)
- **A — Works in dev, missing in the packaged app.** Optional libraries never installed; tests run where everything is installed. Guard: staged-app resolve check + packaged smoke with every agent.
- **B — Same-version assumption.** Data from another version (zips, schemas, plugin bytes) treated as invalid. Guard: real exports from previous releases kept in the repo and imported in CI.
- **C — Security scanner false positives on our own files.** Guard: scanner test over every shipped file, plus real keys that must still be refused.
- **D — Feature never exercised the way a user uses it.** UI paths tested with mocks or not at all (Accept → video, retried rows, legends). Guard: real-UI journey per fix, checked in Chrome.
- **E — Dev-environment assumptions** (certs, ports, which app holds the site).
- **F — Moved files collide** (kUInetic folder move). Guard: export of every theme in CI.
- **G — Media shown at one fixed shape** (cropped tiles, blank video preview). Guard: a media fixture set with vertical/tall/odd shapes in UI checks.
- **H — The assistant can't do what a user can do by hand** (Calendly embed, bundled-plugin edits, folder roots), so it hands manual work back to the user. Guard: every user-doable task has an assistant path test.
- **D also covers "fixed in code, never checked in the real UI"** (B1–B3, B7–B9, the confirm card). Guard: no UI bug is closed without a real-UI check.

## Found by plan-mail (2026-11-06), read from source, not run
- Jini `resend` plugin may not send on Jini's own HTTP client: `resend/adapters/resend.mjs:100` calls `send({method, url, ...})`, but the port is `send({request})` (`core/src/primitives/http.ts:62`). It would return a retryable TRANSPORT_ERROR, so no mail leaves. Hidden in Tovu because Tovu's HTTP wrapper accepts the other shape. Plan P5m: failing test first, fix without editing the plugin.
- Plugin JSON has the frozen label `Tovu Mail — Resend API`, which a second product would see.

- 2026-11-07, from the Jini recon, read not run: `apps/website/src/features/webhooks/keyring.env.ts:1-6` has a marker saying remove on/after 2026-11-01. It is overdue. Not fixed.
- 2026-11-07, from the Jini recon, read not run: Jini `cms/comments` relies on the plugin dataModule engine (`packages/cms/src/comments/sql/repo.ts:9`), which exists only in Tovu, so another host cannot create Jini's comments tables. Not fixed; Codex is checking the claim.

- 2026-11-07, CORRECTION to the Jini comments entry above: Codex read the code and found no runtime dependency on the dataModule engine. `packages/cms/src/comments/sql/repo.ts:23-34` takes host tables. The real gap is that Jini ships no turnkey table definitions, so a host writes its own DDL.
- 2026-11-07, Codex, read not run: Jini's unused `packages/server/src/lifecycle/boot-lifecycle.ts:62-74` stops only completed starts after a critical start failure; Tovu's live `server/runtime/lifecycle/boot-lifecycle.ts:124-134` also stops prepared modules. Adopting Jini's copy as is could leak prepared resources. Not fixed; nothing uses Jini's copy today.

Proposed fix is about 3 lines in Jini, needs a release.
- 2026-11-08, same review, not fixed: Jini `packages/platform/src/fs/guarded-reader.ts` appends native error text, which can include an absolute path, to a refusal the model is allowed to see, on single and batch calls. Existing tests pin the current text.
- 2026-11-08, same review, stale doc: Tovu `apps/website/src/features/media/chat-preview.ts` says batches stay plain JSON; they carry up to 4 previews.
- 2026-11-08 UPDATE, batch error text (entry above): more precise cause. Tovu's single-call redaction layer (`withRedactedToolFailures`, secrets blanked, ERR id, reason kept) sits outside the inner executor, so batch items skip it. The first proposed fix hid the whole reason, which is stricter than a single call; replaced by routing batch items through the same redaction.

## 2026-11-10 — found in the Broadcast theme vs kUInetic fact check (Opus, read-only)
- **NOT REPRODUCED by kUInetic (11-10):** clip-path entrances never firing `on:enter` in Chrome. The default threshold is 0, and kUInetic's own demo runs the same string fine in real Chrome. The likely cause is on the theme side: the showcase tabs hide inactive panels (`display:none` when kUInetic binds), or a zero-height host, or the check ran in a background tab. The workaround in `scripts/kuinetic-showcase.js:69-84` is harmless; fix the comment there if it's confirmed.
- **kUInetic, possible bug:** carousel-3d clicks land on the stage plane, not the card; Broadcast resolves clicks itself and drops mouse clicks for 250ms after a scroll (`scripts/broadcast.js:298-332`).
- **kUInetic, against the "phones keep the motion" rule:** `supportsFineHover` turns at least 7 effects into no-ops on touch (interaction.ts, interaction-proximity.ts, three-d, wobble, cursor-trail).
- **Broadcast, motion off on phones:** hero tally hidden ≤640px (`theme.css:489`); monitor `.led` hidden ≤560px (`theme.css:626`); Blueprint height dimension hidden ≤680px (`ch-blueprint.css:405`); scroll tuning buttons-only below (≥901w & ≥640h) or (≥768w & ≥900h) (`broadcast.js:193`); Blueprint leader lines only ≥1024px (`ch-blueprint.js:34`); several hover-only lifts with no touch stand-in.
- **Broadcast, fragile:** the theme writes `--kui-step-position`, which kUInetic also writes ("one writer"); the auto-trail copies kUInetic internals (trail-layer markup, `kui-trail-photo` keyframes).
- **Broadcast, stale comment:** `theme.css:964-966` says phones get the plain grid; they get the drum.

- [owner localhost, 11-10 ~8:20 PM, not fixed] After moving the live tree to main (Jini 0.7.0), boot logs "[plugin-runtime] boot re-attach failed for 'charts-recharts': plugin 'charts-recharts' failed to load (INTEGRITY_FAILED)" on leons-portfolio — the stored install's digest no longer matches; the plugin stays off until reinstalled. Needs an auto-recover (owner rule: user never fixes manually).
- [desktop survey 11-10, not fixed] Desktop IPC hardening gaps: most main-process IPC handlers don't verify the sender, and the sites-home window runs with the renderer sandbox off. desktop-agent lazily loads @jini-ai/core from the daemon's install location (lazy import against owner rule). Planned fix: a desktop-host/ipc layer that checks the sender on every handler (desktop extraction step 1). Report: ADS-memory/.local-artifacts/codex/2026-11-10/desktop-survey.md (local).
- [tool-failure audit 11-10, not fixed; report ADS-memory/.local-artifacts/codex/2026-11-10/tool-failure-audit.md (17 open bugs listed there)] Site chats 10-20..11-10: 1,324 of ~27k tool calls failed (~4.9%). Still open on main: guessed/unknown tool names (`agent_plugin_<id>`, `content_read`+resource) → 403 instead of a clear "unknown tool"; guidance names tools (e.g. load_agent_plugin) that aren't directly callable → "No such tool"; error masking blanks tool/input names and slugs the model needs ("Call [redacted] with {}"); bare HTTP 500 with no body/id (40; nothing in server.log); theme batches editing the same file twice fail "changed during review" (21) — each item locks the file version up front; 300 s Claude Code abort while a card waits on the owner (5); arg leftovers (theme copy sourceId, theme_list_files without themeId, read_tool_result limit >8000); Chrome tab collisions between site agents and other sessions (192, rising); tool names leaking into replies (22 of 205 since 11-03). Not visible in chat data (check in UI): raw `mcp__jini__` prefix, "Declined" twice.
- [extract canary 11-10, not fixed; details in ADS-memory/.local-artifacts/codex/2026-11-10/extract-canary.md (local only)] (B1, security, latent) Jini devops/source-control's commit path is an older port of Tovu's commit-site.ts and lacks some of its safety checks — harden in Jini before Tovu switches to it. (B2) Tovu source-control provider-module.ts hard-codes the "github" vendor id in core (vendor rule). Duplicates: source-control credential-form.ts copies Jini devops/deploy/target-descriptors' parser; credential-setup's sourceControlSaveVault copies vendor-credentials/abort-guarded-repo.
- [extract canary control 11-10, not fixed; details ADS-memory/.local-artifacts/codex/2026-11-10/extract-canary-control.md (local)] (B3, latent, Jini) devops/source-control commit passes the branch in a form the shipped github plugin doesn't read, so a commit through Jini's path would land on the default branch — unused today; must be fixed before Tovu dedupes onto it. (B4) Jini's SourceControlProviderOperations ABI (object form) doesn't match the shipped github plugin (Tovu's positional ABI) — replacing Tovu's types with Jini's would break the plugin.
- [extract canary #2 11-10, not fixed; details ADS-memory/.local-artifacts/codex/2026-11-10/extract-canary-2.md (local)] (S1) source-control credential-setup's hand-rolled abort wrapper doesn't guard `update` or `project.replaceRepo` — use the shared `abortGuardedCredentialRepo`. (S2) `abortGuardedCredentialRepo` leaves `vault.patchMeta` unguarded (low impact). (S3, latent, hardening) vendor-credentials `credentialVault` duplicates platform/secrets `createTovuStoreVault` but its authorization checks and keyring wiring are looser — unify on one helper; no current caller is affected (detail kept local, not in the report). (S4) project-half credential shape exists in 4 copies under different names. (S5) feature cycle vault-adapter → source-control/types → vendor-credentials. (S6) Tovu vs Jini "envelope unchanged" check differs. (S7/S8) Tovu re-parses Jini-private formats (vault AAD, plugin-state row ids) — a Jini format change would cause refusals. (S9) attached-only vault `findDefault` falls back to the only granted row, undocumented. (S10) `site_restart` registered in the project-keys tool domain. (S11) key attach stamps `new Date()` instead of an injected clock.
- [canary compare 11-10, NOT FIXED, user-hittable, MEDIUM; found by blind Codex canary, verified by Opus; details ADS-memory/.local-artifacts/codex/2026-11-10/codex-canary/comparison.md (local)] Admin Source Control page: a saved GitHub key disappears after reload. Save writes to the canonical custom-credential owner (`features/source-control/store.ts:380-383` + composition `source-control-key-binding.ts`) but list/update/delete read only source-control rows → a second save says duplicate, update returns 404, delete returns 204 and does nothing. Fix with a failing test first (save → list → update → delete on the same owner).

- [canary compare 11-10, not fixed, low-medium] Past 100 grants on one site, every shared project key silently disappears from lists (`project-keys/attachments.ts:30-36` + `attached-vault.ts:26`).
- [canary compare 11-10, not fixed, low] Source-control credential update: a race between two reads can undo a concurrent rename (`source-control/store.ts:447-452`). Also: dead exports toCommitFile, isUnderWorkflowPath, buildLoadedSourceControlProvider; Jini db/secrets-vault/repo.sql.ts imports platform at runtime (owner-rule smell).

## ROUND 3 (Opus) — b061 post — round3/claude/b061.md
- **HIGH PRIORITY (owner: wait for the batch)** — HIGH, data loss, schema VERIFIED by coordinator: features/post/agent-tools.ts:620-637 widgetEmbed attrs schema is strict (additionalProperties:false) and lists placementId/content/cssClass/htmlAttributes but NOT widgetEntryId, which Jini cms embed-service collectEmbeds requires to recognize the node. The schema's own comment says strict MCP validation makes a model strip unlisted fields → an agent re-sending a post/page body that contains a widget drops widgetEntryId → the embed stops rendering. Known and left unfixed in duplicate-embeds.ts:8-17. Fix: add widgetEntryId to the schema (keep-when-present) + test.

Jini ext-j1 (merged in Jini merge-5 f479e406) extracted the taxonomy purge/trash hooks with SCALAR repo calls: `cms/src/taxonomy/taxonomy-trash-follow-ups.ts:65,72` `findForPurgeAudit(id: string)` / `listIdsForPurgeAudit(taxonomyId: string)` (called :108,157) and `taxonomy/trash-term.ts:51,56` `findForTrash(id: string)` (called :82,107). The Tovu donor and Jini's own SQL adapters use object inputs (`{ id }`). If a host wires the Jini SQL repo into these hooks, the lookups receive a string where an object is expected. Tovu still uses its own donor copy, so nothing is broken in Tovu today. Fix: align the Jini hooks to the object shape (failing test first), then run K-05b-T.

- 2026-11-21 (tovu-1b, owner report from a client site) [OPEN, fix dispatched → codex/tv-howto-pages] Built-from-an-image page went into widgets instead of page HTML: the client uploaded a design image, the assistant placed the content as widget instances, so the page editor HTML and Interactive tabs were blank (confusing). Cause: bundled plugin `content/agent-plugins/how-tovu-works/skills/how-tovu-works/SKILL.md:10` tells the AI to "reuse ... widget instances/regions ... before proposing custom code", and neither SKILL.md nor `tovu-site-guide.json` says when to use a page vs a post vs a widget. Owner rule: a page built from a design/image is a PAGE whose HTML holds the layout (widgets only for reusable pieces embedded by marker); complex/designed layouts default to pages, never posts (posts = dated articles).

Incident 3 refined: same-tab clean editor already refreshes; the loop happens with another tab/window, the Interactive tab (always counted dirty), or a refresh settling during Save.

Most likely: the load effect in `apps/admin/src/features/posts/hooks/use-post-editor.hooks.ts:878-917` calls `applyLoadedPost` → `editor.commands.setContent` (:868) while the TipTap editor from `useEditor` (:683, @tiptap/react 3.x returns Editor | null) is still null; the `.catch` at :916 shows the raw JS message (violates no-generic-errors rule). Body never loads → risk that Save writes an empty body over the real post. Other unguarded `editor.commands` uses: :559, :1275, :1302.
- 2026-11-21 (tovu-1b) [FIX PUSHED codex/tv-post-editor-null 3e0eb2a34d, awaits bug-batch merge] Post-editor "reading commands" root cause confirmed: @tiptap/react 3.27 useEditor destroys its first editor after a 1 ms timer when effects run late; the load effect wrote the body into the destroyed editor (commandManager=null). CONFIRMED DATA LOSS RISK: on base, Publish in that state stored an EMPTY body (v3→v4); Save asked "Nothing changed. Save anyway?" then wrote. Two old tests had locked in the unguarded save. Fix: useLoadedPostApply + isLiveEditor, save/publish refuse until body applied, plain load errors (21 locales). Tests 5 red → 9/9 green; 3 mutations red.
